!important;}.has-more-pro-font-family{font-family: var(--wp--preset--font-family--more-pro) !important;}
/*The Events Calendar - Event URL Line Break*/
.tribe-events-event-url {
    overflow-wrap: break-word;
}

.gw-mm-item__dropdown {
	padding: 0;
}

/*Nav*/
.menu-btn{
	text-decoration:none!important;
}
.utility-nav{
	column-gap:.75rem;
	color:#777470;
}
.main-nav{
	column-gap:.5rem;
}

/*News Slider*/
.kb-splide .splide__arrow{
	border-radius:50px;
	background-color:#B5121B;
	height:2.5rem;
	width:2.5rem;
	opacity:1;
}
.kb-splide .splide__arrow:focus,.kb-splide .splide__arrow:hover {
	background-color:#B5121B;
	opacity:.75;
}
.news-loop>.kt-inside-inner-col{
	display:flex;
	align-items:center;
	gap:8px;
}
.news-carousel .splide__arrow--prev{
	margin-left:-2rem;
	margin-top:-.5rem;
}
.news-carousel .splide__arrow--next{
	margin-right:-2rem;
	margin-top:-.5rem;
}
.mobile-nav>.kt-row-column-wrap{
	align-items:center;
}

/*Sticky Posts on Home*/
.sticky-feat-img{
	margin-bottom:0!important;
}

/*Post Hover*/
.wp-block-query .post-img, .wp-block-post-featured-image{
	overflow:hidden;
}
.wp-block-query .post-img img, .wp-block-post-featured-image img{
	transition: .5s ease all;
}
.wp-block-query .post-img img:hover, .wp-block-post-featured-image img:hover{
	transform: scale(1.1);
}
.tribe-events-schedule{
	margin-bottom:0!important;
}

/*Post Excerpt Styles*/
.wp-block-post-author-name a{
	font-size: 0.75rem;
	color:#262626;
}
.hls-hover_underline p{
	margin-bottom:0;
}
.wp-block-post-author-name a:hover{
	color:#262626;
}
.kb-section-has-overlay .wp-block-post-author-name a, .kb-section-has-overlay .wp-block-post-author-name a:hover{
	color:#ffffff;
}
.wp-block-query .wp-block-kadence-dynamiclist{
	line-height:16px!important;
}

/*Topic Columns Mobile Home*/
@media only screen and (max-width: 767px){
	.core-topic-col>.kt-inside-inner-col{
		display:flex;
		flex-direction:row;
	}
	.core-topic-col>.kt-inside-inner-col figure{
		width: 50%;
    margin-right: 1rem;
	}
}	

/*Pagination*/
.wp-block-query-pagination-numbers .current{
padding: .2rem .6rem;
border: 1px solid;
border-radius: 50px;
color:#B5121B!important;
}

.wp-block-query-pagination-numbers .page-numbers{
  color:#777470;
  margin-left:6px;
}

/*Mobile Typography*/

@media only screen and (max-width: 767px){

h1{
font-family: ff-more-web-pro, serif;
font-size: 2.1rem;
font-style: normal;
font-weight: 500;
line-height: 2.7rem;
}

h2{
font-family: ff-more-web-pro, serif;
font-size: 1.75rem;
font-style: normal;
font-weight: 500;
line-height: 2.125rem;
}

h3{
font-family: ff-more-web-pro, serif;
font-size: 1.25rem;
font-style: normal;
font-weight: 500;
line-height: 1.625rem;
}

h4{
font-family: ff-more-web-pro, serif;
font-size: 1.2 rem;
font-style: normal;
font-weight: 500;
line-height: 2 rem;
}


h5{
font-family: ff-more-web-pro, serif;
font-size: 1rem;
font-style: normal;
font-weight: 500;
line-height: 1.25rem;
	}}

.btn-no-underline a:hover{
	text-decoration:none;
}

.entry-content li{
	margin-bottom:.5rem;
}

.entry-content .wp-block-query li{
	margin-bottom:0rem;
}

/*Team*/
.team figure{
	overflow:hidden;
}
.team .kb-section-has-link figure img{
	transition: .5s ease all;
}
.team .kb-section-has-link:hover figure img{
	transform: scale(1.1);
}

/*Calendar*/
.tribe-common h1, .tribe-common h2, .tribe-common h3, .tribe-common h4, .tribe-common h5{
	font-weight: 500!important;
}
.tribe-events-event-meta .tribe-events-meta-group{
	width:100%!important;
}
abbr[data-original-title], abbr[title]{
	text-decoration:none!important;
}
.single-tribe_events .tribe-events-event-meta{
	margin-top:0!important;
}

/*MISC*/
blockquote:before{
	  font-size: 6rem!important;
    color: #B5121B;
    margin-top: 1rem;
}
.hidden-link{
	display:none;
}

.tribe-events-calendar-month {
	max-width: 1200px;
	margin-left: auto;
	margin-right: auto;
}

/*External Posts*/

.wp-block-query h5 a[target=_blank]::after, .term-posts h3 a[target=_blank]::after {
content:url(/wp-content/uploads/2023/10/ArrowUpRightScaleSmallThick.svg);
	padding-left:.3rem
}

/*Glossary*/
.glossary-block ul{
	padding-left:0;
}
.glossary-block li{
	list-style: none;
    margin-bottom: 2rem;
}
.glossary-link-item .glossary-list-term-excerpt{
	display:block;
}
.glossary-term-bar{
	margin-bottom: 2rem;
}
.glossary-cats-list{
	list-style: none;
    padding-left: 0;
}
.glossary-search-terms form{
	display: grid;
    grid-gap: 16px;
    grid-template-columns: 3fr 1fr;
}
.glossary-search-terms select{
	grid-column: 1/3;
	padding: 0.5rem;
    border: 1px solid #dee2e6;
	border-radius:25px;
}
@media only screen and (min-width:800px){
.glossary-search-terms input[type="text"]{
	min-width:500px;
	}}
.glossary-search-terms input[type="submit"]{
	border-radius:50px!important;
}
.sidebar-glossary-search .glossary-search-terms input[type="text"]{
	min-width:inherit!important;
}

.sidebar-glossary-search .glossary-search-terms select{
	display:none;
}

/*FilterEverything Styles*/
.wpc-filters-widget-select{
	padding:1rem 1.2rem 1rem 1.2rem;
	color:#3B3B3B;
	border-color:#dddad7;
	font-size:14px;
}
.wpc-filter-content select{
	border-radius:50px;
}

.wpc-search-field {
	height: 54px!important;
}
.wpc-filter-header{
	display:none;
}
.wpc-filter-set-widget-title h2{
	font-size:12px;
	color:#777470;
	font-family: Inter;
	font-weight:bold;
}

/*Home Carousel*/
.news-carousel .kadence-post-image-inner-intrisic{
	border-radius:100px!important;
}
.news-carousel .entry-title a{
	font-family: More Pro;
	font-weight: 500;
	color:#3B3B3B;
}
.news-carousel .kt-post-grid-layout-carousel-wrap{
	padding: 1.2rem 3rem;
}
/*Matching Field UI and Post UI*/
.filter-wrapper .filter .filter-list-wrapper ul.filter-list li.filter-item.active .filter-link, .filter-wrapper .filter .term-posts-search-form-wrapper form.term-posts-search-form #term-posts-search, .filter-wrapper .filter .filter-list-wrapper ul.filter-list li.filter-item .filter-link{
	font-weight:400!important;
	color:#3b3b3b;
}
.filter-wrapper .filter .filter-label-wrapper .filter-label{
	font-weight:400!important;
}
.term-text, .author-name{
	font-weight:600!important;
}
.author-name{
	color:#262626;
}

/*Responsive - Mobile*/
@media only screen and (max-width: 1124px){
	.block-term-posts .filter{
		flex-direction:column;
	}
	.block-term-posts .filter-label-wrapper{
		margin-bottom:1rem;
	}
	.block-term-posts .term-posts-search-form-wrapper{
		margin-left: 0!important;
    margin-top: 4rem;
	}
	.block-term-posts #term-posts-search, .block-term-posts .filter-list-wrapper, .filter-wrapper .filter .filter-list-wrapper ul.filter-list li.filter-item.active{
		width:100%!important;
	}
	.term-post{
		flex-wrap:nowrap!important;
		column-gap:18px!important;
	}
	.term-post-col-1{
		flex-basis:40%!important;
	}
	.wpc-filters-widget-wrapper{
		flex-direction:column;
	}
	.wpc-filters-section{
		margin-bottom:0.5em!important;
	}
	.wpc-horizontal-layout.wpc-horizontal-cols-2 .wpc-filters-section:not(.wpc-filter-layout-submit-button){
		width:100%!important;
		max-width:100%!important;
	}
}

/*Misc*/
.back-button a p.wp-block-kadence-advancedheading{
	margin-bottom:0!important;
}
.kb-section-is-sticky>.kt-inside-inner-col{
	top:5rem;
}

.gfield--type-captcha label {
	display: none !important;
}

@media (max-width: 980px) {
  .wp-block-getwid-megamenu .gw-mm__wrapper .gw-mm__content-wrapper {
    margin-top: 27px !important;
  }
}
@media (min-width: 900px) {
.blog .kb-advanced-slider .slick-prev{
	margin-left:-2rem;
	}

.blog .kb-advanced-slider .slick-next{
	margin-right:-2rem;
	}}

[id*=term-posts-block_] .term-posts-wrapper .term-posts .term-post-wrapper .term-post .terms-wrapper ul.terms:not(:last-of-type):after, [id*=term-posts-block_] .term-posts-wrapper .term-posts .term-post-wrapper .term-post .terms-wrapper ul.terms li.term:not(:last-of-type):after{
	font-size:7px!important;
}
.single-tribe_events .tribe-events-event-meta .tribe-events-meta-group{
	padding:1rem;
}

/*Ads*/
.ad-wrapper img {
	max-width: 100%;
	height: auto;
}

/*Events and Calendar*/
.tribe-events-header__breadcrumbs{display:none;}
.tribe-events-header__title{display:none;}
.tribe-events-header, .tribe-events-calendar-list-nav, .post-type-archive-tribe_events .tribe-events-c-subscribe-dropdown__container{
	padding-left:2rem!important;
	padding-right:2rem!important;
}
.tribe-common .tribe-common-g-row{align-content: flex-start;
}
.past-events .wp-block-post-template-is-layout-grid{
	gap:2rem;
}
.tribe-events-widget-events-list__view-more{
	display:none;
}
.tribe-events-event-meta{
	border: 0!important;
  background-color: #EAF5F3!important;
}
@media (max-width: 768px) {
	#tribe-events-subscribe-dropdown-content{
		position: relative;
    top: 0px;
	}
}

/*Health Beat Kadence Carousel*/
.img-post-carousel .splide__slide .kt-blocks-post-grid-item-inner header{
	display:flex;
	flex-wrap:wrap;
	position:absolute;
	bottom:0;
	padding-bottom:2rem;
	padding-left:3rem;
	padding-right:3rem;
  z-index: 2;
}

.img-post-carousel .splide__slide .kt-blocks-post-grid-item-inner header .entry-title{
	order:1;
}

.img-post-carousel .kt-blocks-post-grid-item img{
	transition: .5s ease all;
}

.img-post-carousel .kadence-post-image-inner-intrisic::before {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  top: 0;
  left: 0;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(6,47,64,0) 40%, rgba(6,47,64,.9) 70%);
  pointer-events: none;
	z-index: 1; 
}
.img-post-carousel .kadence-post-image-inner-intrisic:hover::before {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  top: 0;
  left: 0;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(6,47,64,0) 40%, rgba(6,47,64,.9) 70%); 
  pointer-events: none;
}

.img-post-carousel .kt-blocks-post-grid-item:hover img{
 transform: scale(1.1);
}

.img-post-carousel .kt-blocks-date-updated{
	display:none;
}
.img-post-carousel .kt-blocks-post-top-meta{
	order:2;
}
.img-post-carousel .kt-blocks-post-grid-item .entry-title a{
	font-family: ff-more-web-pro, serif!important;
font-style: normal!important;
font-weight: 500!important;
}
@media only screen and (min-width: 992px){
	.img-post-carousel .splide__track{
	margin-left:-30%;
	margin-right:-30%;
	}}
@media only screen and (max-width:800px){
	.img-post-carousel .kadence-post-image-inner-intrisic::before {
  background: rgba(6,47,64,.8)!important;
}
}
/*Misc Mobile*/
	@media only screen and (max-width:400px){
		.terms-wrapper{
			display:none!important;
		}
	}	

/*Health Beat Fix*/	
.blog .kt-blocks-post-grid-item-inner header{
	flex-direction:column;
} 

.single-contest-entry .contest-entry-links .contest-entry-link a.contest-entry-link-button {
	word-wrap: break-word; 
}


.wpc-filters-section-18966s {
	display: none;
}

</style>
<style id='core-block-supports-inline-css'>
.wp-container-core-navigation-is-layout-3817c3ed{justify-content:flex-end;}

</style>
<style id='wp-block-template-skip-link-inline-css'>

		.skip-link.screen-reader-text {
			border: 0;
			clip-path: inset(50%);
			height: 1px;
			margin: -1px;
			overflow: hidden;
			padding: 0;
			position: absolute !important;
			width: 1px;
			word-wrap: normal !important;
		}

		.skip-link.screen-reader-text:focus {
			background-color: #eee;
			clip-path: none;
			color: #444;
			display: block;
			font-size: 1em;
			height: auto;
			left: 5px;
			line-height: normal;
			padding: 15px 23px 14px;
			text-decoration: none;
			top: 5px;
			width: auto;
			z-index: 100000;
		}
/*# sourceURL=wp-block-template-skip-link-inline-css */
</style>

<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column726_ee4a5c-d7 kb-section-dir-vertical" data-aos="fade-up" data-aos-duration="800" data-aos-easing="ease-out-quart" data-aos-once="true"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<p class="kt-adv-heading726_d5ec31-ba wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading726_d5ec31-ba"><a href="https://healthjournalism.org/about/">About</a></p>



<h1 class="kt-adv-heading726_8d0fd1-47 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading has-secondarydk-color has-text-color" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading726_8d0fd1-47" data-aos-duration="500" data-aos-easing="ease-out-quart" data-aos-once="true">Principles and Policies</h1>



<h4 class="kt-adv-heading726_e131cc-90 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading has-secondary-color has-text-color" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading726_e131cc-90" data-aos-duration="500" data-aos-delay="100" data-aos-easing="ease-out-quart" data-aos-once="true">How we move health care journalism forward.</h4>
</div></div>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column726_f7058a-5a"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-image kb-image726_9ef789-37" data-aos="zoom-out" data-aos-duration="600" data-aos-easing="ease-out-cubic" data-aos-once="true"><figure class="aligncenter size-full"><img decoding="async" width="904" height="603" src="https://healthjournalism.org/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/ahcj-about-policies.webp" alt="" class="kb-img wp-image-12069" srcset="https://healthjournalism.org/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/ahcj-about-policies.webp 904w, https://healthjournalism.org/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/ahcj-about-policies-300x200.webp 300w, https://healthjournalism.org/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/ahcj-about-policies-768x512.webp 768w, https://healthjournalism.org/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/ahcj-about-policies-375x250.webp 375w" sizes="(max-width: 904px) 100vw, 904px" /></figure></div>
</div></div>

</div></div>

<div class="kb-row-layout-wrap kb-row-layout-id726_81469a-fe alignfull wp-block-kadence-rowlayout"><div class="kt-row-column-wrap kt-has-1-columns kt-row-layout-equal kt-tab-layout-inherit kt-mobile-layout-row kt-row-valign-top kb-theme-content-width">

<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column726_0edebb-02"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<h3 class="kt-adv-heading726_ff4b02-f1 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading has-secondarydk-color has-text-color" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading726_ff4b02-f1">Our mission is to improve public understanding of health care issues by advancing the quality, accuracy and visibility of health care journalism. We work with thousands of journalists each year to improve the quality, accuracy and visibility of health care reporting and editing internationally.</h3>



<div style="height:32px" aria-hidden="true" class="wp-block-spacer"></div>



<h4 class="kt-adv-heading726_a81b96-f5 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading726_a81b96-f5">Our goals:</h4>


<div class="kb-row-layout-wrap kb-row-layout-id726_601361-d7 alignfull wp-block-kadence-rowlayout"><div class="kt-row-column-wrap kt-has-4-columns kt-row-layout-equal kt-tab-layout-inherit kt-mobile-layout-row kt-row-valign-top kb-theme-content-width">

<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column726_26b85c-fe kb-section-dir-horizontal"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column726_cf98d2-91 kb-section-dir-vertical"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<h3 class="kt-adv-heading726_a51d14-e3 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading has-light-color has-text-color" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading726_a51d14-e3">1</h3>
</div></div>



<p><strong>To support</strong> the highest standards of reporting, writing, editing, and broadcasting in health care journalism for the general public and trade publications.</p>
</div></div>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column726_129cfd-c3 kb-section-dir-horizontal"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column726_f8230a-85 kb-section-dir-vertical"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<h3 class="kt-adv-heading726_3f76bc-92 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading has-light-color has-text-color" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading726_3f76bc-92">2</h3>
</div></div>



<p><strong>To develop</strong> a strong and vibrant community of journalists concerned with all forms of health care journalism.</p>
</div></div>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column726_27f208-90 kb-section-dir-horizontal"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column726_4cd259-07 kb-section-dir-vertical"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<h3 class="kt-adv-heading726_e9f7de-db wp-block-kadence-advancedheading has-light-color has-text-color" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading726_e9f7de-db">3</h3>
</div></div>



<p><strong>To raise</strong> the stature of health care journalism in newsrooms, the industry, and the public, as a whole.</p>
</div></div>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column726_e366d0-65 kb-section-dir-horizontal"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column726_b3d89b-3e kb-section-dir-vertical"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<h3 class="kt-adv-heading726_f72dff-cd wp-block-kadence-advancedheading has-light-color has-text-color" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading726_f72dff-cd">4</h3>
</div></div>



<p><strong>To promote</strong> understanding between journalists and sources of news about how each can best serve the public.</p>
</div></div>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column726_356722-7c kb-section-dir-horizontal"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column726_c384f5-fe kb-section-dir-vertical"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<h3 class="kt-adv-heading726_a491f0-f4 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading has-light-color has-text-color" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading726_a491f0-f4">5</h3>
</div></div>



<p><strong>To advocate</strong> for the free flow of information to the public.</p>
</div></div>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column726_af4370-bc kb-section-dir-horizontal"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column726_6ea92b-57 kb-section-dir-vertical"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<h3 class="kt-adv-heading726_1fa5bb-9d wp-block-kadence-advancedheading has-light-color has-text-color" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading726_1fa5bb-9d">6</h3>
</div></div>



<p><strong>To advocate</strong> for the improvement of professional development opportunities for journalists who cover any aspect of health and health care.</p>
</div></div>

</div></div></div></div>

</div></div>

<div class="kb-row-layout-wrap kb-row-layout-id726_5aee2a-75 alignfull kt-row-has-bg wp-block-kadence-rowlayout"><div class="kt-row-column-wrap kt-has-1-columns kt-row-layout-equal kt-tab-layout-inherit kt-mobile-layout-row kt-row-valign-top kb-theme-content-width">

<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column726_f447a3-92"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<h2 class="kt-adv-heading726_ace8bd-4b wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading726_ace8bd-4b">These are the principles and policies that enable us to achieve our goals.</h2>


<div class="kb-row-layout-wrap kb-row-layout-id726_09e736-b9 alignfull wp-block-kadence-rowlayout"><div class="kt-row-column-wrap kt-has-3-columns kt-row-layout-equal kt-tab-layout-inherit kt-mobile-layout-row kt-row-valign-top kt-inner-column-height-full kb-theme-content-width">

<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column726_8d4165-8d kb-section-has-link"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<h4 class="kt-adv-heading726_e16672-fe wp-block-kadence-advancedheading has-kb-palette-9-color has-text-color" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading726_e16672-fe">Bylaws</h4>



<p class="has-kb-palette-9-color has-text-color">Find details about AHCJ membership, its board of directors, membership meetings and other important business.</p>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-icon kt-svg-icons kt-svg-icons726_8faef1-c9 alignnone">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-single-icon kt-svg-style-default kt-svg-icon-wrap kt-svg-item-726_653582-bc"><span class="kb-svg-icon-wrap kb-svg-icon-fe_arrowRight"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"  fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"  aria-hidden="true"><line x1="5" y1="12" x2="19" y2="12"/><polyline points="12 5 19 12 12 19"/></svg></span></div>
</div>
</div><a href="https://healthjournalism.org/about/principles-and-policies/bylaws-of-association-of-health-care-journalists/" class="kb-section-link-overlay"></a></div>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column726_11db4a-9e kb-section-has-link"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<h4 class="kt-adv-heading726_4756e8-7e wp-block-kadence-advancedheading has-kb-palette-9-color has-text-color" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading726_4756e8-7e">Statement of Principles</h4>



<p class="has-kb-palette-9-color has-text-color">Learn how AHCJ helps journalists covering health care issues meet their unique challenges.</p>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-icon kt-svg-icons kt-svg-icons726_f0d470-7c alignnone kb-icon-valign-bottom">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-single-icon kt-svg-style-default kt-svg-icon-wrap kt-svg-item-726_20aa82-74"><span class="kb-svg-icon-wrap kb-svg-icon-fe_arrowRight"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"  fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"  aria-hidden="true"><line x1="5" y1="12" x2="19" y2="12"/><polyline points="12 5 19 12 12 19"/></svg></span></div>
</div>
</div><a href="https://healthjournalism.org/about/principles-and-policies/statement-of-principles-of-the-association-of-health-care-journalists/" class="kb-section-link-overlay"></a></div>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column726_139f40-b3 kb-section-has-link"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<h4 class="kt-adv-heading726_3770c7-c0 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading has-kb-palette-9-color has-text-color" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading726_3770c7-c0">Self-Promotion and Exploitation</h4>



<p class="has-kb-palette-9-color has-text-color">Learn why journalists should avoid featuring their own actions or spotlighting people they may help.</p>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-icon kt-svg-icons kt-svg-icons726_d91c04-6e alignnone">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-single-icon kt-svg-style-default kt-svg-icon-wrap kt-svg-item-726_9e5ee9-91"><span class="kb-svg-icon-wrap kb-svg-icon-fe_arrowRight"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"  fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"  aria-hidden="true"><line x1="5" y1="12" x2="19" y2="12"/><polyline points="12 5 19 12 12 19"/></svg></span></div>
</div>
</div><a href="https://healthjournalism.org/about/principles-and-policies/self-promotion-and-exploitation/" class="kb-section-link-overlay"></a></div>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column726_3f2120-1e kb-section-has-link"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<h4 class="kt-adv-heading726_b95bb0-4e wp-block-kadence-advancedheading has-kb-palette-9-color has-text-color" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading726_b95bb0-4e">Corrections Policy</h4>



<p class="has-kb-palette-9-color has-text-color">Learn how our approach to corrections helps us achieve our goal to communicate objectively and precisely.</p>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-icon kt-svg-icons kt-svg-icons726_abf8fc-40 alignnone kb-icon-valign-bottom">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-single-icon kt-svg-style-default kt-svg-icon-wrap kt-svg-item-726_d1701e-79"><span class="kb-svg-icon-wrap kb-svg-icon-fe_arrowRight"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"  fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"  aria-hidden="true"><line x1="5" y1="12" x2="19" y2="12"/><polyline points="12 5 19 12 12 19"/></svg></span></div>
</div>
</div><a href="https://healthjournalism.org/about/principles-and-policies/corrections-policy/" class="kb-section-link-overlay"></a></div>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column726_bf5d6e-0b kb-section-has-link"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<h4 class="kt-adv-heading726_134218-0a wp-block-kadence-advancedheading has-kb-palette-9-color has-text-color" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading726_134218-0a">Conflicts of Interest Policy</h4>



<p class="has-kb-palette-9-color has-text-color">See how the AHCJ’s board of directors and executive committee handle and disclose possible conflicts of interest.</p>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-icon kt-svg-icons kt-svg-icons726_93ea05-84 alignnone">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-single-icon kt-svg-style-default kt-svg-icon-wrap kt-svg-item-726_cb8a38-17"><span class="kb-svg-icon-wrap kb-svg-icon-fe_arrowRight"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"  fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"  aria-hidden="true"><line x1="5" y1="12" x2="19" y2="12"/><polyline points="12 5 19 12 12 19"/></svg></span></div>
</div>
</div><a href="https://healthjournalism.org/about/principles-and-policies/conflicts-of-interest-policy/" class="kb-section-link-overlay"></a></div>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column726_632eb4-ad kb-section-has-link"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<h4 class="kt-adv-heading726_e0866e-63 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading has-kb-palette-9-color has-text-color" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading726_e0866e-63">Fundraising Policy</h4>



<p class="has-kb-palette-9-color has-text-color">Read how our ethical approach to fundraising minimizes the possibility and appearance of inappropriate influence.</p>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-icon kt-svg-icons kt-svg-icons726_0b145c-a2 alignnone">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-single-icon kt-svg-style-default kt-svg-icon-wrap kt-svg-item-726_1ac572-cc"><span class="kb-svg-icon-wrap kb-svg-icon-fe_arrowRight"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"  fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"  aria-hidden="true"><line x1="5" y1="12" x2="19" y2="12"/><polyline points="12 5 19 12 12 19"/></svg></span></div>
</div>
</div><a href="https://healthjournalism.org/about/principles-and-policies/fundraising-policy/" class="kb-section-link-overlay"></a></div>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column726_5a6579-50 kb-section-has-link"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<h4 class="kt-adv-heading726_db1c23-c6 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading has-kb-palette-9-color has-text-color" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading726_db1c23-c6">Republishing Policy</h4>



<p class="has-kb-palette-9-color has-text-color">Follow the guidelines for sharing content, stories and art from AHCJ.</p>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-icon kt-svg-icons kt-svg-icons726_6a8bc3-48 alignnone">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-single-icon kt-svg-style-default kt-svg-icon-wrap kt-svg-item-726_3f4b09-78"><span class="kb-svg-icon-wrap kb-svg-icon-fe_arrowRight"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"  fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"  aria-hidden="true"><line x1="5" y1="12" x2="19" y2="12"/><polyline points="12 5 19 12 12 19"/></svg></span></div>
</div>
</div><a href="https://healthjournalism.org/about/principles-and-policies/republishing-policy/" class="kb-section-link-overlay"></a></div>

<h4 class="kt-adv-heading726_f6c2a2-f3 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading has-kb-palette-9-color has-text-color" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading726_f6c2a2-f3">Government Transparency Policy</h4>



<p class="has-kb-palette-9-color has-text-color">Read our recommendations on the release of information concerning deaths, epidemics or emerging diseases.</p>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-icon kt-svg-icons kt-svg-icons726_597dc2-51 alignnone">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-single-icon kt-svg-style-default kt-svg-icon-wrap kt-svg-item-726_e484c9-ca"><span class="kb-svg-icon-wrap kb-svg-icon-fe_arrowRight"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"  fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"  aria-hidden="true"><line x1="5" y1="12" x2="19" y2="12"/><polyline points="12 5 19 12 12 19"/></svg></span></div>
</div>

<p class="has-kb-palette-9-color has-text-color">See our guidelines for journalists and public health officials to consult when decisions must be made about what information should be released.</p>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-icon kt-svg-icons kt-svg-icons726_000fdf-21 alignnone">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-single-icon kt-svg-style-default kt-svg-icon-wrap kt-svg-item-726_2fa8e9-51"><span class="kb-svg-icon-wrap kb-svg-icon-fe_arrowRight"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"  fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"  aria-hidden="true"><line x1="5" y1="12" x2="19" y2="12"/><polyline points="12 5 19 12 12 19"/></svg></span></div>
</div>
</div><a href="https://healthjournalism.org/about/principles-and-policies/release-guidance/" class="kb-section-link-overlay"></a></div>

<script id="wp-block-template-skip-link-js-after">
	( function() {
		var skipLinkTarget = document.querySelector( 'main' ),
			sibling,
			skipLinkTargetID,
			skipLink;

		// Early exit if a skip-link target can't be located.
		if ( ! skipLinkTarget ) {
			return;
		}

		/*
		 * Get the site wrapper.
		 * The skip-link will be injected in the beginning of it.
		 */
		sibling = document.querySelector( '.wp-site-blocks' );

		// Early exit if the root element was not found.
		if ( ! sibling ) {
			return;
		}

		// Get the skip-link target's ID, and generate one if it doesn't exist.
		skipLinkTargetID = skipLinkTarget.id;
		if ( ! skipLinkTargetID ) {
			skipLinkTargetID = 'wp--skip-link--target';
			skipLinkTarget.id = skipLinkTargetID;
		}

		// Create the skip link.
		skipLink = document.createElement( 'a' );
		skipLink.classList.add( 'skip-link', 'screen-reader-text' );
		skipLink.id = 'wp-skip-link';
		skipLink.href = '#' + skipLinkTargetID;
		skipLink.innerText = 'Skip to content';

		// Inject the skip link.
		sibling.parentElement.insertBefore( skipLink, sibling );
	}() );
	
//# sourceURL=wp-block-template-skip-link-js-after
</script>
